{"id": "6d992d34120a0c16", "timestamp_utc": "2026-09-27T03:55:15.541741+00:00", "author": "max3", "kind": "conclusion", "claim": "C82 exact elimination of the ENTIRE C81 two-parameter triangle family: a,b,c>1,d>=0 admits an explicit radical/piecewise-quadratic necessary test E_min(h)>=0, with no remaining witness parameter and no numerical search. Includes all coefficient signs, boundary limits, and the unbounded-negative case. In1<a<=2,1<b<=1+sqrt3 the scalar minimum is at an explicit endpoint. Full C81 geometry and exact record100 rejection are reproduced in the SAME joint proof; no unverified finding is used as a fact.", "evidence": "# C81: a four-step triangle with an interior-edge query\n\nStatus: complete new joint UNVERIFIED construction. No verifier call is\nallocated. This is analytical continuation, cumulative round19/19, of\nthe CLOSED C79 discovery allocation. No optimization, candidate sweep,\nor exact numerical replay is performed here. The only fixed schedule\nspecialized below is the already saved record100.\n\nThe accepted fact04219554e41d9750, Section10, supplies the original\nprimitive energy and its full certificate identity. All new geometry and\nenergy calculations below are proved directly rather than imported from\nthe unverified C79/C80 drafts.\n\n## 1. General actual function and four-step trajectory\n\nLet h=(a,b,c,d), with a>1, b>1, c>1, d>=0. Define\n\n B=b-1, C=c-1, D=d+1, s=a+b+c+d, A=1+s.\n\nChoose u>0 and z>0 satisfying\n\n u>=B,\n z^2 <= (u+1)((a-1)u-1),\n z^2 >= C d(D-BC)/D^2.                                  (1)\n\nThe second inequality implies (a-1)u-1>0. The third imposes\nno additional restriction if its right side is negative. Put\n\n p=(u,0), q=(-1,z), r=(-BC/D,0), K=conv{p,q,r},\n F(x)=max_(g in K) {<x,g>-||g||^2/2}.                      (2)\n\nThis nondegenerate compact triangle contains0 in the relative interior\nof its horizontal base. Completing the square shows that the unique\nmaximizer in (2) is the Euclidean projection P_K(x).\n\nFor completeness, if p_x=P_K(x), p_y=P_K(y), the two projection\nnormal inequalities give\n\n ||p_x-p_y||^2 <= <p_x-p_y,x-y>.\n\nHence P_K is1-Lipschitz. The maximizing formula gives\n\n 0<=F(x+t)-F(x)-<p_x,t>\n       <=<P_K(x+t)-p_x,t><=||t||^2,\n\nso F is differentiable with gradient P_K; it is convex as a supremum\nof affine functions. Since0 belongs to K, F>=0 and F(0)=0. Moreover\nP_K(x)=0 exactly when x is in the normal cone of K at0. Pairing x\nwith both signs of the horizontal base forces its first coordinate\nto vanish; pairing it with q then forces its second coordinate<=0.\nThus argmin F={(0,t):t<=0}.\n\nThe proposed ORIGINAL iterates and gradients are\n\n x0=(a u-1,z),       g0=(u,0),\n x1=(-1,z),         g1=(-1,z),\n x2=(B,-Bz),        g2=(B,0),\n x3=(-BC,-Bz),      g3=(-BC/D,0),\n x4=(-BC/D,-Bz),    g4=(-BC/D,0).                        (3)\n\nThe four equations x_(i+1)=x_i-h_i g_i follow by subtraction.\nThe initial point has positive second coordinate, so its closest\nminimizer is0 and its squared distance is(a u-1)^2+z^2.\n\nWe check every required projection normal, including both terminal\norientations implicitly through the single actual function.\n\nAt x0, the residual y0=x0-g0=((a-1)u-1,z) has\n\n <q-p,y0>=z^2-(u+1)((a-1)u-1)<=0,\n <r-p,y0>=(-BC/D-u)((a-1)u-1)<0.\n\nAt x1 the residual is0. At x2, g2 is on the base because\n-BC/D<0<B<=u, and y2=x2-g2=(0,-Bz) has zero pairing with\nboth base directions and pairing-Bz^2<0 with q-g2.\n\nAt x3 the residual is y3=(-BCd/D,-Bz). Its base comparison\nwith p-r is nonpositive, and\n\n <q-r,y3>=B{C d(D-BC)/D^2-z^2}<=0.\n\nAt x4 the residual y4=(0,-Bz) has zero pairing with the base\nand pairing-Bz^2<0 with q-r. These are all vertices of K,\nso (3) gives the actual gradient of the same F at every query.\n\nFor all five indices define f_i=F(x_i)=<x_i,g_i>-||g_i||^2/2.\nEvery original ordered interpolation residual has the exact form\n\n Q_ij=f_i-f_j+<g_i,x_j-x_i>+||g_i-g_j||^2/2\n     =<g_i-g_j,x_j-g_j><=0.                              (4)\n\nThis follows from the proved projection normals. The optimum sample0\nalso obeys all ordered comparisons. Thus no guessed rank or deleted\ngradient is needed to certify legality.\n\n## 2. Exact intrinsic energy and a necessary family of inequalities\n\nUse the energy from accepted04219554e41d9750, Section10:\n\n V_h=2sum_(i=0)^3 h_i(f_i-f4)\n       +sum_(i=0)^3 h_i(1-h_i)||g_i||^2-s(s+1)||g4||^2.\n\nThe identity\n\n ||x_i||^2-||x_(i+1)||^2\n     =2h_i f_i+h_i(1-h_i)||g_i||^2\n\nfollows from (3) and f_i=<x_i,g_i>-||g_i||^2/2.\nSumming it gives\n\n V_h=||x0||^2-||x4||^2-2s f4-s(s+1)||g4||^2.\n\nHere f4=||g4||^2/2, ||x4||^2=(BC/D)^2+B^2z^2, and therefore\n\n V_h=(a u-1)^2+(1-B^2)z^2-A^2(BC/D)^2.                 (5)\n\nConsequently the original full primitive cone must satisfy\n\n (a u-1)^2+(1-B^2)z^2 >= A^2(BC/D)^2                  (6)\n\nfor EVERY u,z obeying(1). Indeed its accepted exact identity is\nV_h+sum Lambda_ij Q_ij=<M,Gram(g)>, where Lambda_ij>=0,\nthe flow holds, and M is PSD in all five original gradients.\nThe right side is nonnegative and every Q_ij<=0, so it implies V_h>=0.\nA negative value in(5) contradicts this identity directly.\n\nThis is a necessary family, not a sufficient condition. The imposed\na,b,c>1 is a scope assumption of the witness and is NOT a lossless\nrestriction of the full four-step search domain.\n\n## 3. Exact rejection of saved discovery record100\n\nKeep the already proposed schedule\n\n h=(8/5,2,471/250,309/250),   s=168/25,   A=193/25.\n\nThus B=1,C=221/250,D=559/250. Choose u=2,z=3/5.\nThe upper inequality in(1) is\n\n z^2=9/25 <=3/5=(u+1)((a-1)u-1).\n\nFor its lower inequality,\n\n C d(D-C)/D^2\n    =(221*309*338)/(250*559^2) < 9/25,\n\nbecause 221*309*338=23081682 <28123290=90*559^2.\nAll numbers in(2)--(4) are therefore rational. Explicitly,\n\n K=conv{(2,0),(-1,3/5),(-221/559,0)},\n\n x0=(11/5,3/5),   g0=(2,0),\n x1=(-1,3/5),     g1=(-1,3/5),\n x2=(1,-3/5),     g2=(1,0),\n x3=(-221/250,-3/5), g3=(-221/559,0),\n x4=(-221/559,-3/5), g4=(-221/559,0).\n\nThe exact values, if desired independently of(4), are\n\n f0=12/5, f1=17/25, f2=1/2,\n f3=(d+1/2)(221/559)^2, f4=(221/559)^2/2.\n\nEquation(5), with B=1, gives\n\n V_h=(11/5)^2-(193*221/(25*559))^2\n    =-(11908*73398)/(625*559^2)<0.                        (7)\n\nThe comparison is exact:193*221=42653>30745=55*559.\nThe initial closest-minimizer distance squared is26/5. The terminal\ngradient/value and the displayed negative energy concern the intrinsic\nprimitive criterion. They do not assert an objective minimax theorem.\n\nThis rejects the precise candidate left in the round18 handoff.\nThe handoff's numerical rank/value observations were only motivation;\nno numerical Gram, tolerance, PSD repair or approximate identity is a\npremise of this proof. No new schedule has been proposed or evaluated.\n\n## 4. Exact one-parameter elimination when b=2\n\nFor1<a<=2, b=2, c>1 and d>0 with D>C, put\n\n L_81=C d(D-C)/D^2>0,\n u_81=[2-a+sqrt(a^2+4(a-1)L_81)]/[2(a-1)].\n\nThe function H(u)=(u+1)((a-1)u-1) is strictly increasing for\nu>=1/(a-1); its derivative there is at least a>0. Its positive\nroot for H(u)=L_81 is exactly u_81. Because L_81>0,\nu_81>1/(a-1)>=1. Hence u=u_81,z=sqrt(L_81) is allowed in(1).\nEvery full primitive certificate in this regime therefore forces\n\n a u_81-1 >= A C/D.                                    (8)\n\nThe left side is positive, so(8) is equivalent to(6) in this\nparticular specialization. This explicitly eliminates the witness\nparameters; it is not a complete classification of b=2 schedules,\nlet alone of all four-step schedules.\n\n## 5. Retrieval and correctness boundary\n\nBefore proving, fact_search found the accepted conditional bridge and\nthe general interpolation fact1252b8823bb5a290, but no accepted version\nof(1)--(8). Shared-memory search found C80 and older projection-lift\ndrafts, whose claims are not used as facts here.\n\nThe required Matlas query returned three unrelated descent results;\nnone is used. The existing exact local copy of Taylor--Hendrickx--Glineur,\narXiv1502.05666, Sections2.4--2.5 and the proof of Theorem4, was reread\nfor the conjugate construction. All needed projection/differentiability\nclaims are proved above, so this candidate imports no external theorem.\n\nThe original four-step existence question and original asymptotic\ntarget remain OPEN. Accepted04219554e41d9750 gives the conditional lift\nif a different actual full seed is found. No fact_submit is authorized\nor performed for this draft.\n\n\n# C82: exact elimination of the C81 witness parameters\n\nStatus: UNVERIFIED joint extension. For the complete construction and\nproof, concatenate the C81 proof with this file. C81 is not an accepted\nfact; its geometry is part of the joint proof, not an imported premise.\nThere are no new word proposals, optimizer calls or numerical replays.\n\nLet h=(a,b,c,d), a,b,c>1,d>=0, and put\n\n alpha=a-1, B=b-1, C=c-1, D=d+1, A=1+a+b+c+d,\n H(u)=(u+1)(alpha u-1),\n ell=max{0,C d(D-BC)/D^2},\n R=[2-a+sqrt(a^2+4alpha ell)]/(2alpha),\n U=max{B,R}, K0=A^2(BC/D)^2.\n\nDefine three scalar coefficients\n\n q2=a^2+(1-B^2)alpha,\n q1=-2a+(1-B^2)(a-2),\n q0=B^2-K0,\n\nand q(u)=q2 u^2+q1 u+q0.\n\n## 1. Feasible parameter domain\n\nThe C81 triangle has parameters u>=B,z>0 with\n\n ell<=z^2<=H(u).\n\nSince alpha>0, H has its only nonnegative root at1/alpha;\nH'(u)=2alpha u+a-2>=a>0 for u>=1/alpha.\nSolving H(u)=ell therefore gives R>=1/alpha exactly as above.\nThe closure of the admissible parameter domain is\n\n u>=U, ell<=z^2<=H(u).                                  (1)\n\nEvery boundary point in(1) with z=0 is a limit of points with\nu>U,z>0 obeying the original normal inequalities. In particular\nH(u)>ell for u>U. Taking such a limit in the explicit polynomial\nenergy is justified by continuity; no assertion about strict normals\nat the limiting point is needed.\n\n## 2. Closed formula for the infimum over this entire witness family\n\nThe energy is\n\n E(u,z)=(a u-1)^2+(1-B^2)z^2-K0.\n\nIf0<B<=1, its smallest value at fixed u uses z^2=ell.\nMoreover a u-1>0 throughout u>=U>=1/alpha, so that value is\nstrictly increasing in u. Its infimum over the family is\n\n E_min=(a U-1)^2+(1-B^2)ell-K0.                         (2)\n\nIf B>1, its smallest value at fixed u uses z^2=H(u).\nExpanding the resulting expression gives exactly q(u). Hence:\n\n * If q2<0, or q2=0 and q1<0, then E_min=-infinity.\n * If q2=0 and q1>=0, then E_min=q(U).\n * If q2>0, set u_opt=max{U,-q1/(2q2)}. Then\n   E_min=q(u_opt).                                      (3)\n\nThese statements follow respectively from the leading term, from\nlinearity on the half-line, and from completing the square\nq(u)=q2(u+q1/(2q2))^2+q0-q1^2/(4q2).\n\nEvery original full primitive certificate with a,b,c>1,d>=0\ntherefore must satisfy E_min>=0, with E_min defined by(2)--(3).\nIf E_min<0, an actual finite u and z>0 give a negative energy:\nfor an attained strict negative value this follows directly or by\ncontinuity from a zero-z boundary; for E_min=-infinity take a\nsufficiently large finite u. The C81 normals then give an actual\nconvex1-smooth function with a minimizer. This is a completely\nparameter-eliminated necessary test for this particular family.\n\n## 3. A broad monotone regime\n\nSuppose1<a<=2 and1<b<=1+sqrt(3). If B<=1, formula(2)\nalready applies. If1<B<=sqrt(3), then\n\n q2>=a^2-2(a-1)=(a-1)^2+1>0,\n q'(1/alpha)=a[2/alpha+1-B^2]>=0,\n\nbecause alpha<=1 and B^2<=3. Since q2>0, q' increases;\nthus q is nondecreasing throughout u>=U>=1/alpha.\nConsequently no stationary-point alternative is needed in this\nregime: E_min=q(U) if B>1. If additionally R>=B, then H(U)=ell\nand BOTH cases reduce to the same explicit radical inequality\n\n (a R-1)^2+(1-B^2)ell >= A^2(BC/D)^2.                    (4)\n\nAt B=1 it specializes to Section4 of C81. These are conditional\nparameter statements. No unverified global-domain reduction is used\nto claim that an arbitrary four-step seed lies in this regime.\n\n## 4. Exact scope of the remaining gap\n\nEliminating the witnesses is not eliminating the original twenty\nnonnegative multipliers. The displayed tests are necessary because\neach negative case yields an actual trajectory; their nonnegative\ncases certify only that this particular triangle family cannot reject\nthe schedule. No converse to the full flow/PSD interface is proved.\nIn particular failure to derive a contradiction from(2)--(4) does not\ngive a seed, and a negative saved optimization score does not give a\nglobal impossibility theorem.\n", "verifiable": true, "status": "unverified", "fact_id": null, "links": {"artifact": "research/max3_c82_joint_triangle_and_elimination.md", "fact_ids": ["04219554e41d9750"], "related_finding": "618a43a2e5b13506", "plan_id": "max3_round19_symbolic_triangle_closure", "status": "complete_joint_candidate_unverified", "justification_type": "explicit_construction_and_calculation", "is_fragile": false, "fragility_reason": "", "suggested_followup": "none", "scope": "Exact necessary test for a defined witness family; no sufficiency or global seed impossibility", "new_model_evaluations": 0, "new_word_proposals": 0, "new_fact_submit": 0}, "glossary": {"E_min": "Infimum of the exactly specified C81 energy over all its allowed witness parameters; formulas(2)--(3) of C82.", "H_82": "H(u)=(u+1)((a-1)u-1).", "ell_82": "max{0,(c-1)d[(d+1)-(b-1)(c-1)]/(d+1)^2}.", "R_82": "Nonnegative solution of H_82(R)=ell_82, explicitly given by a quadratic radical.", "U_82": "max{b-1,R_82}, the smallest allowed first-gradient parameter.", "q2_82": "a^2+(1-(b-1)^2)(a-1), the leading coefficient after z elimination.", "q1_82": "-2a+(1-(b-1)^2)(a-2).", "q0_82": "(b-1)^2-(1+a+b+c+d)^2[((b-1)(c-1))/(d+1)]^2."}}
{"id": "749c14575c110748", "timestamp_utc": "2026-09-27T03:56:59.243730+00:00", "author": "max2", "kind": "conclusion", "claim": "NEW joint DRAFT backward extension of C80: for every remaining four-step seed h=(1+a,1+b,1+c,1+d), the full-pair triangle strengthens to bc[R+b(P+C)]<=D J, where P=2+a,D=2+d,H=C-2-a-b, eta=b/H, kappa_plus=max((1+a)(a²+2a-1)/a,0), R=P²-kappa_plus, J=P²-kappa_plus(1+eta). It strictly strengthens the old triangle whenever h0>sqrt(2). No numerical discovery or verifier call.", "evidence": "## 15. A backward extension strengthens the middle triangle\n\nThis is a new joint DRAFT calculation in the seventh launch's final round.\nIt is not a fact and consumes no verifier or numerical allowance. All four\nsteps retain their order. Write\n\n    h=(1+a,1+b,1+c,1+d), a,b,c,d>0,\n    C=5+a+b+c+d, P=2+a, D=2+d, U=C-P,\n    H=U-b=1+c+D, eta=b/H,\n    kappa(a)=(1+a)(a^2+2a-1)/a,\n    kappa_plus=max(kappa(a),0),\n    R=P^2-kappa_plus, J=P^2-kappa_plus*(1+eta).\n\nThe preceding full-domain derivation gives a<1 and the b-cut gives\neta<=P/C, hence a*eta<1 (indeed a*P/C<3/7). Then a stronger necessary\ninequality than the middle adjacent triangle is\n\n    b*c*[R+b(P+C)] <= D*J.                           (43)\n\nFor a<=sqrt(2)-1 this is exactly(34). For a>sqrt(2)-1 the positive\nterm subtracted from the old triangle slack is strictly positive.\nNo relation between c and b, or saturation assumption, is imposed.\n\nHere is the entire new interpolation check. The case D<=b*c is already\nexcluded by the explicit capped function in Section11. Thus D>b*c. Set\n\n    K=b^2*c+U*(D-b*c)=U*D-b*c*H,\n    r=b^2*c/K,\n    u=(1,0,0), w=(r,sqrt(r*(1-r)),0),\n    v=-(b*u+D*w)/(1+c).\n\nWe have 0<r<1, <u,w>=||w||^2=r, and 0 lies in conv{u,v,w}.\nThese are exactly the middle triangle vectors, but its initial prefix\nwill now change. Choose t in[-eta,1] and put\n\n    q=(1+(a-1)*t)/a,\n    z=t*u+sqrt(q-t^2)*(0,0,1).\n\nThe radicand is (1-t)(1+a*t)/a>=0 because a*eta<1. Define the actual\npositions and gradients by\n\n    x=(u+(1+a)*z, u, -b*u, D*w, w),\n    g=(z,u,v,w,w),\n    f_i=<x_i,g_i>-||g_i||^2/2.\n\nThe four GD equalities hold. Add the optimum jet(0,0,0). Each proximal\npoint y_i=x_i-g_i must support conv{z,u,v,w} at g_i. This suffices for\nall ordered Q because Q_ij=<g_i-g_j,y_j>.\n\nAt i=0, y_0=u+a*z. Since t+a*q=1+a*t and every old vertex y in\n{0,u,v,w} has <u,y><=1,\n\n    <y-z,y_0>=(1+a*t)(<u,y>-1)<=0.\n\nAt i=1 and i=4 the proximal point is zero. At i=3 it equals(1+d)*w.\nIts pairing with z-w is (1+d)*r*(t-1)<=0; pairings with u-w and v-w\nare respectively zero and negative, as explicitly computed in Section11.\n\nAt i=2 let n=-b*u-v. The two old vertex comparisons are\n\n    <w-v,n>=0, <u-v,n>=-b*(1-r)*c/(1+c)<0.\n\nThe remaining comparison with z is exact. Put A_num=D*r-b*c. Then\n\n    <u,n>=A_num/(1+c),\n    <v,n>=r*(D-b*c)/(1+c),\n    A_num=-b*c*H*(D-b*c)/K<0,\n    r*(D-b*c)=-eta*A_num.\n\nThe fresh coordinate of z is orthogonal to n. Consequently\n\n    <z-v,n>=(t+eta)*A_num/(1+c)<=0.\n\nThe optimum comparisons follow as 0 is already a convex combination\nof u,v,w. This checks every original ordered pair, including those\ninvolving the modified initial gradient. Fact1252b8823bb5a290 realizes\nthe complete array by one global convex1-smooth function with minimizer0.\nNo gradient Gram from any other schedule has been reused.\n\nThe energy is ||x_0||^2-C^2*r by the directly derived identity(3), and\n\n    ||x_0||^2\n      =1+2(1+a)t+(1+a)^2*q\n      =P^2+kappa(a)*(t-1).\n\nChoose t=1 if kappa(a)<=0 and t=-eta otherwise. The initial squared\nnorm is exactly J. Nonnegative energy therefore requires\n\n    C^2*b^2*c <= J*K.\n\nUsing K=U*D-b*c*H, C-P=U, and J*H=P^2*H-kappa_plus*U gives\n\n    C^2*b^2*c-J*K\n      =U*{b*c*[P^2-kappa_plus+b(P+C)]-D*J},\n\nwhich proves(43). Compared to the original triangle's forbidden-side\nexpression, the new left-minus-right expression increases by\n\n    kappa_plus*[D*(1+eta)-b*c]\n      =kappa_plus*K/H.\n\nThis is strictly positive whenever a>sqrt(2)-1. Thus(43) is a genuine\nstrengthening throughout that region, rather than a relabeling of the\nold test. This last algebraic comparison is a strength comparison only;\nit does not by itself prove there exists a word passing every old test\nand failing the new one.\n\nFor rational elimination on the a>sqrt(2)-1 branch, use\n\n    R=a+3+1/a,\n    J=R-kappa(a)*b/(C-2-a-b),\n    c <= J*(C-3-a-b)/(J+b*[R+b(C+2+a)]),              (44)\n\nwhere c+d=C-5-a-b and D=2+d. All denominators are positive: H>0,\nJ=||x_0||^2>0, b>0, R>0. The strict positivity of J follows also\nbecause its fresh coordinate is nonzero at t=-eta. Equations(43),(44)\nretain the independent variables C,a,b,d (with c recovered by the sum).\n", "verifiable": true, "status": "unverified", "fact_id": null, "links": {"artifact": "workers/max2/research/four_step_seed_compact_domain.md Section15", "related_findings": ["81720922d2d132d7", "7b447329aac48edd"], "fact_ids": ["1252b8823bb5a290"], "conditional_bridge_fact": "04219554e41d9750", "status": "complete_joint_proof_draft_unverified", "new_optimizer": 0, "new_fact_submit": 0}, "glossary": {"a": "First step minus1 in this finding.", "b": "Second step minus1 in this finding.", "c": "Third step minus1 in this finding.", "d": "Fourth step minus1 in this finding.", "P": "2+a, the previous constant-gradient prefix capacity.", "D": "2+d, the constant-gradient suffix capacity.", "C": "1+sum h=5+a+b+c+d.", "H": "C-2-a-b=1+c+D.", "eta": "b/H.", "kappa_plus": "Positive part of(1+a)(a^2+2a-1)/a.", "R": "P^2-kappa_plus.", "J": "P^2-kappa_plus(1+eta), the squared initial norm of the new legal witness."}}
{"id": "32380fa0da39d710", "timestamp_utc": "2026-09-27T03:59:41.800578+00:00", "author": "max3", "kind": "conclusion", "claim": "C83 new ALL-CURVATURE full-pair cut repair: for any nonnegative word and any selected h_j>1, every kappa in(0,1] gives an exact necessary inequality beta V0+eta(1+eta)V_kappa>=0. Beta uses the NEGATIVE curvature residual credit, so its reciprocal gives the exact maximal repair weight of the stated orthogonal base/auxiliary construction. A negative case yields an actual planar convex1-smooth function with a minimizer. For n4 middle cuts j=1,2 the entire continuum becomes explicit polynomial branches of degree at most5,7. Unit-curvature repair rederived as a special case; no search/evaluation or unverified premise.", "evidence": "# C83: exact all-curvature repair at a cut\n\nStatus: complete joint UNVERIFIED analytical candidate. No new schedule,\noptimizer/model evaluation, numerical replay, installation or verifier\ncall. This strengthens the shared unit-curvature cut/quad idea by using\nevery curvature kappa in(0,1], retaining its strictly negative original\ninterpolation residuals, and deriving the exact maximal repair weight.\nThe unit-curvature shared draft is motivation only; everything needed\nis rederived here. Accepted facts used:1252b8823bb5a290 (interpolation)\nand04219554e41d9750 Section10 (full primitive certificate identity).\n\n## 1. Data and statement of the necessary condition\n\nLet h=(h_0,...,h_(n-1)) be any finite nonnegative word, let\ns=sum h_i,A=1+s, and select an index j with h_j>1. Define\n\n P=sum_(i<j)h_i, R=sum_(i>j)h_i, H=P+h_j=s-R,\n eta=(h_j-1)/(1+R)>0, C0=eta(1+eta),\n V0=(1+P)^2-A^2 eta^2.\n\nFor kappa in(0,1] let\n\n p_0=1, p_i=product_(t<i)(1-kappa h_t) for0<=i<=j+1,\n E=1+kappa R, q=kappa p_(j+1)/E,\n t_kappa=(1-kappa)/(2kappa).\n\nIf R>0 put\n\n beta=max{0, max_(0<=i<=j)\n       [q(kappa p_i-q)-t_kappa(kappa p_i-q)^2/R]}.\n\nIf R=0 put beta=0. Finally define\n\n V_kappa=\n   kappa sum_(i=0)^j h_i[1+kappa(1-h_i)]p_i^2\n   -H[s+R+1+1/kappa]q^2.                               (1)\n\nEvery full primitive certificate for the ORIGINAL word necessarily\nsatisfies, for every such j and every0<kappa<=1,\n\n             beta V0+C0 V_kappa >=0.                    (2)\n\nFor beta=0 the stronger explicit interpretation is V_kappa>=0,\nwhich is equivalent to(2) because C0>0. No other step is assumed\npositive, above1, symmetric, ordered or part of a primitive prefix.\n\n## 2. The legal scalar cut coordinate\n\nUse the scalar convex function with derivative clip(x,-eta,1),\nminimum0 at0, and initial point1+P. Put\nP_i=sum_(t<i)h_t and R_i=sum_(t>=i)h_t. Its actual data are\n\n x_i^0=1+P-P_i,    g_i^0=1,             i<=j,\n x_i^0=-eta(1+R_i), g_i^0=-eta,         i>j,\n f_i^0=x_i^0 g_i^0-(g_i^0)^2/2.\n\nThe selected step sends1 to1-h_j=-eta(1+R), and the remaining\nupdates keep the points on the negative constant-gradient segment,\nending at-eta. All zero steps retain their original sample indices.\nThis verifies the full GD chronology on one actual function.\n\nFor this coordinate the ordered residual is\n\n Q_ik^0=(g_i^0-g_k^0)(x_k^0-g_k^0).\n\nIt is0 inside either block. From the prefix to a suffix target k>j,\nit is exactly-C0 R_k. In the opposite direction it is\n-(1+eta)(P-P_k)<=0. All optimum comparisons are nonpositive.\nThe norm-telescoping identity, or direct substitution, gives its\nprimitive energy V0 exactly as defined above.\n\n## 3. A quadratic prefix and constant-gradient suffix\n\nDefine a second set of scalar data, not yet claimed to be legal:\n\n x_i^1=p_i, g_i^1=kappa p_i, f_i^1=kappa p_i^2/2,       i<=j;\n x_i^1=q(1/kappa+R_i), g_i^1=q,\n f_i^1=q x_i^1-q^2/(2kappa),                           i>j.\n\nThe point after the selected step is p_(j+1)=q(1/kappa+R),\nso these data follow every original GD update and end at q/kappa.\nInclude the optimum sample (x_star^1,g_star^1,f_star^1)=(0,0,0).\n\nWrite Q_ik^1=f_i^1-f_k^1+g_i^1(x_k^1-x_i^1)\n                  +(g_i^1-g_k^1)^2/2.\nDirect substitution gives the COMPLETE list of residual types:\n\n * i,k<=j:\n   Q_ik^1=-t_kappa(g_i^1-g_k^1)^2<=0.\n * i,k>j: Q_ik^1=0.\n * i>j,k<=j:\n   Q_ik^1=-t_kappa(q-kappa p_k)^2<=0.\n * i<=j,k>j:\n   Q_ik^1=R_k q(kappa p_i-q)\n                    -t_kappa(kappa p_i-q)^2.\n * Q_(i,star)^1=-t_kappa(g_i^1)^2<=0 for all i.\n * Q_(star,i)^1=-t_kappa(g_i^1)^2 for i<=j, and\n   Q_(star,i)^1=-(R_i+t_kappa)q^2 for i>j.\n\nThus only the fourth type can be positive. If R_k=0 it is\nnonpositive. For R_k>0, its quotient by R_k is\n\n q(kappa p_i-q)-t_kappa(kappa p_i-q)^2/R_k,\n\nwhich is nondecreasing as R_k increases. Since0<R_k<=R,\nits largest possible value is at k=j+1, whose R_k equals R.\nThis proves that beta is the exact largest nonnegative ratio of\na possibly bad residual to its corresponding suffix mass.\n\nIn particular beta=0 makes ALL auxiliary data, including optimum\ncomparisons, legal. If R=0 there are no positive suffix masses,\nso the convention beta=0 has exactly this same meaning.\n\n## 4. Orthogonal repair gives an actual globally legal function\n\nIf beta>0 choose epsilon=C0/beta and set\n\n X_i=(x_i^0,sqrt(epsilon)x_i^1),\n G_i=(g_i^0,sqrt(epsilon)g_i^1),\n F_i=f_i^0+epsilon f_i^1,\n\nwith the zero optimum sample. GD still uses the same full word.\nEvery original residual is Q_ik^0+epsilon Q_ik^1. All types except\nprefix-to-suffix are sums of nonpositive numbers. For that type\nwith R_k>0 the sum is at most\n\n       -C0 R_k+epsilon beta R_k=0;\n\nwhen R_k=0 the auxiliary residual itself is nonpositive. Therefore\nall directed comparisons, including the optimum and original terminal\nsample, hold. A maximizing i in beta and k=j+1 show that epsilon\nis also the largest permitted weight of this fixed auxiliary data\nagainst this fixed base coordinate. No residual was discarded.\n\nBy accepted1252b8823bb5a290, proof paragraph2, these planar jets have\nan everywhere finite convex differentiable1-smooth interpolant with\nminimum value0 at0. Concretely, with I={star,0,...,n}, define\n\n c_i=<G_i,X_i>-F_i,\n J_i(v)=c_i+<X_i,v-G_i>+||v-G_i||^2/2,\n J(v)=max_(i in I) J_i(v),\n F(x)=sup_v{<x,v>-J(v)}.\n\nThe cited fact proves F(X_i)=F_i and grad F(X_i)=G_i from exactly\nthe residual convention just verified. Thus these are actual GD data\nof one function, not a sum of separately illegal functions.\n\nIf beta=0, apply the same accepted interpolation construction directly\nto the auxiliary scalar jets, since their full residual list is legal.\nNo division by beta occurs in this case.\n\n## 5. Exact energy calculation\n\nFor the auxiliary suffix, f_i^1-f_n^1=q^2 R_i and\nf_n^1=q^2/(2kappa). The elementary finite identity\n\n 2sum_(i>j) h_i R_i = R^2+sum_(i>j)h_i^2\n\nfollows by expanding R^2. Thus the combined suffix contribution of\nthe weighted value differences and gradient diagonals is R(R+1)q^2.\nThe prefix contribution is\n\n kappa sum_(i<=j)h_i[1+kappa(1-h_i)]p_i^2-H q^2/kappa.\n\nSubtracting the terminal s(s+1)q^2 gives(1), because\ns(s+1)-R(R+1)=H(s+R+1).\n\nThe primitive energy is linear in all values and Gram entries.\nThe repaired planar trajectory therefore has energy\n\n          V0+(C0/beta)V_kappa\n\nwhen beta>0, and the legal auxiliary trajectory has energy V_kappa\nwhen beta=0. Accepted04219554e41d9750, Section10, says an original\nfull nonnegative flow/PSD certificate forces nonnegative energy on\nevery actual trajectory. This proves(2), including beta=0.\n\n## 6. The unit-curvature slice and exact low-degree polynomial interface\n\nAt kappa=1, q=p_(j+1)/(1+R)=-eta p_j, t_kappa=0, and\n\n beta=max{0,max_(i<=j)q(p_i-q)}, V_1=1-A^2 q^2.\n\nFor the last identity, the prefix sum in(1) telescopes to\n1-p_(j+1)^2, using p_(i+1)^2=(1-h_i)^2 p_i^2. Then\n(1+R)^2+H(s+R+2)=A^2. This rederives the unit-curvature\nrepair without treating the shared max2 draft as a correctness premise.\n\nFor R>0 the entire curvature interval can be expressed through\nfinitely many POLYNOMIAL branches. Define\n\n d_i(kappa)=[E p_i-p_(j+1)]/kappa,                     0<=i<=j,\n T_i(kappa)=2R p_(j+1)d_i-(1-kappa)d_i^2,\n T_max(kappa)=max{0,T_0(kappa),...,T_j(kappa)},\n\n P_cut(kappa)=E^2 sum_(i<=j)h_i[1+kappa(1-h_i)]p_i^2\n              -H[1+kappa(s+R+1)]p_(j+1)^2.\n\nThe numerator defining d_i vanishes at kappa=0, so d_i is a\npolynomial, including at0. It has degree at most j. Consequently\nT_i has degree at most2j+1, and P_cut degree at most2j+3.\nThe exact identities are\n\n beta=kappa^3 T_max/(2R E^2),\n V_kappa=kappa P_cut/E^2.\n\nSince kappa,R,E are positive, (2) is equivalent to\n\n kappa^2 V0 T_max(kappa)+2R C0 P_cut(kappa)>=0,\n                                             0<kappa<=1. (3)\n\nAt each branch where0 or a specific T_i is the maximum, (3) is\na polynomial inequality of degree at most2j+3. For a four-step word,\nthe two middle cuts j=1,2 therefore require degrees at most5,7.\nTheir branch selection inequalities are the explicit comparisons\nT_i>=0 and T_i>=T_k, with the zero branch selected when all T_i<=0.\nThis gives a precise exact real-algebraic interface for further work;\nno polynomial-root search or numerical interval scan is performed.\n\n## 7. Scope and remaining task\n\nThe construction gives legal adversaries whenever(2) fails. Passing\nall these tests gives no full certificate; no sufficiency is claimed.\nThe convex combination mechanism retains every original interpolation\npair and all function-value offsets. A favorable or unfavorable outcome\non any finite list of curvatures would not settle the universal interval.\n\nNo new word was selected, tested, rounded, or evaluated in deriving this\nfamily. It neither constructs the missing four-step seed nor excludes\nevery h with sum>=168/25. The accepted conditional lift remains available\nas fact04219554e41d9750; the seed existence problem is still OPEN.\n", "verifiable": true, "status": "unverified", "fact_id": null, "links": {"artifact": "research/max3_c83_all_curvature_cut_repair.md", "fact_ids": ["1252b8823bb5a290", "04219554e41d9750"], "related_unit_curvature_draft": "81720922d2d132d7", "plan_id": "max3_round19_symbolic_triangle_closure", "status": "complete_joint_candidate_unverified", "scope": "Necessary condition for all original nonnegative schedules at each cut>1; neither sufficiency nor global seed impossibility", "justification_type": "explicit_all_pair_construction_and_calculation", "is_fragile": false, "fragility_reason": "", "suggested_followup": "none", "new_optimization": 0, "new_word_proposals": 0, "new_fact_submit": 0}, "glossary": {"kappa_83": "Auxiliary curvature in(0,1]; written kappa within this finding.", "V0_83": "Primitive energy (1+P)^2-(1+sum h)^2 eta^2 of the exact scalar cut coordinate.", "V_kappa_83": "Exact auxiliary energy in equation(1), for a curvature-kappa quadratic prefix and a constant-gradient suffix.", "beta_83": "Maximum nonnegative ratio of an auxiliary potentially positive interpolation residual to its suffix mass; explicit formula Section1.", "eta_83": "Selected cut gradient magnitude (h_j-1)/(1+R), R the sum after the selected cut.", "p_i_83": "Ordered product of(1-kappa h_t) over t<i in the selected prefix.", "q_83": "Auxiliary terminal gradient kappa p_(j+1)/(1+kappa R).", "C0_83": "eta_83(1+eta_83), the exact base residual repair coefficient.", "P_cut_83": "Polynomial E^2 sum_(i<=j)h_i[1+kappa(1-h_i)]p_i^2-H[1+kappa(s+R+1)]p_(j+1)^2.", "T_max_83": "Maximum of0 and the finitely many explicit branch polynomials T_i from Section6."}}
{"id": "a7c38e9640fbd13c", "timestamp_utc": "2026-09-27T04:10:37.190117+00:00", "author": "max2", "kind": "conclusion", "claim": "NEW joint DRAFT last-pair triangle with a two-step prefix: retain g0=g1=z but both original initial normals, obtaining C² c²d<=J_pre K_tail with q_pre=max(q_late,q_early). This strictly improves the old last triangle on every remaining branch h0<=sqrt2 and h1>=2. Complete all-pair proof; it is not a seed or a global exclusion.", "evidence": "## 16. A second extension retains both initial gradients and their normals\n\nContinue the same joint draft, with h=(1+a,1+b,1+c,1+d) and the same\npositive a,b,c,d. This section constructs a different legal witness,\nwith its last triangle at steps2 and3. Its symbols are local to this\nsection and do not change the definitions in Section15.\n\nThe old adjacent triangle at the middle pair implies b*c<2+d, and the\nlast adjacent triangle implies c*d<1. Set\n\n    U_tail=c+d+2,\n    K_tail=c^2*d+U_tail*(1-c*d),\n    r_tail=c^2*d/K_tail,\n    eta_tail=c/(d+2),\n    A_pre=1+a+b, H_pre=A_pre+1=2+a+b,\n    Z_tail=(c+r_tail)/(1+d),\n    q_late=(1+(1-b)*eta_tail)/b,\n    q_early=[eta_tail+(A_pre*eta_tail-1)*Z_tail]/A_pre,\n    q_pre=max(q_late,q_early),\n    J_pre=1-2H_pre*eta_tail+H_pre^2*q_pre.\n\nEvery denominator is positive. We have 0<r_tail<1, b*eta_tail<1, and\n\n    q_late-eta_tail^2\n      =(1+eta_tail)(1-b*eta_tail)/b>0.\n\nThe resulting new necessary inequality is\n\n    C^2*c^2*d <= J_pre*K_tail.                       (45)\n\nThe old last-pair triangle is retained too. Equivalently the right\nside can be replaced by min(J_pre,(3+a+b)^2)*K_tail. This use of a\nminimum means choosing the stronger of two independently legal witnesses.\nIt imposes no equality between formal gradients in the full certificate.\n\nFor the complete construction take\n\n    u=(1,0,0),\n    w=(r_tail,sqrt(r_tail*(1-r_tail)),0),\n    v=-(c*u+w)/(1+d),\n    z=-eta_tail*u+sqrt(q_pre-eta_tail^2)*(0,0,1).\n\nAs before 0 lies in conv{u,v,w}. Define all five original samples by\n\n    x=(u+H_pre*z, u+(1+b)*z, u, -c*u, w),\n    g=(z,z,u,v,w),\n    f_i=<x_i,g_i>-||g_i||^2/2,\n\nand add the zero optimum jet. Each GD step has exactly its original\nlength. We check the complete normal list for conv{z,u,v,w}.\n\nAt indices2 and4 the normal is zero. At index3 it is n=-c*u-v.\nThe triangle calculation gives\n\n    <w-v,n>=0,\n    <u-v,n>=-c*(1-r_tail)*d/(1+d)<0,\n    <z-v,n>=0.\n\nFor the last equality the Section15 calculation uses first-pair\nshift c, second-pair shift d, suffix capacity1, and t=-eta_tail.\nIt is independent of q_pre because the fresh coordinate is orthogonal\nto n. Explicitly, with B_num=r_tail-c*d<0,\n\n    <u,n>=B_num/(1+d),\n    <v,n>=r_tail*(1-c*d)/(1+d),\n    r_tail*(1-c*d)=-eta_tail*B_num.\n\nAt index1 the normal is u+b*z. For an old vertex y in{0,u,v,w},\n\n    <y-z,u+b*z>\n      =(1-b*eta_tail)<u,y>+eta_tail-b*q_pre.\n\nThe coefficient of <u,y> is positive and its maximum over the old\nvertices is at u. The choice q_pre>=q_late makes that maximum<=0.\n\nAt index0 the normal is u+A_pre*z. Its comparison with y is\n\n    (1-A_pre*eta_tail)<u,y>+eta_tail-A_pre*q_pre.\n\nThe old values <u,y> lie between -Z_tail (at v) and1 (at u).\nThe v comparison is nonpositive by q_pre>=q_early. At u, even the\nsmaller q_late gives the exact value\n\n    (1+eta_tail)*(1-A_pre/b)<0,\n\nsince A_pre>b. These two endpoints control the entire interval,\nincluding0 andw, for either sign of1-A_pre*eta_tail. The pairs\nbetween indices0 and1 have the same gradient and hence Q=0. This\nexhausts all original directed and optimum interpolation inequalities.\nFact1252b8823bb5a290 supplies the single global convex1-smooth function.\n\nThe saturated-value energy identity gives\n\n    V=||x_0||^2-C^2||w||^2=J_pre-C^2*r_tail.\n\nThis proves(45) without a numerical Gram or a loss of any original\ncoefficient freedom. If it is violated, the displayed data constitute\nan explicit full-pair legal negative-V witness in three dimensions.\n\nThere is a useful closed subregion where the max disappears. If\nA_pre*eta_tail<=1, the earliest normal has a nonnegative u coefficient,\nso the calculation above proves q_early<=q_late. In that subregion put\n\n    kappa_pre=H_pre*[2b+H_pre*(b-1)]/b.\n\nThen\n\n    J_pre=(3+a+b)^2-kappa_pre*(1+eta_tail).            (46)\n\nThus the old last-pair triangle is strictly strengthened whenever\nA_pre*eta_tail<=1 and b>H_pre/(H_pre+2).\n\nIn particular this happens throughout the branch\n\n    0<a<=sqrt(2)-1, b>=1                            (47)\n\nof any remaining candidate. To check this assertion without numerical\nconstants, write P=2+a, L_mid=(P+C)/P^2. The old middle triangle says\nb*eta_tail<=1/(1+L_mid*b). On(47), a<1/2 and P<5/2, so\n\n    L_mid>(2+193/25)/(25/4)=972/625>3/2>1+a.\n\nConsequently b+L_mid*b^2> b+1+a=A_pre, proving A_pre*eta_tail<1.\nAlso b>=1>H_pre/(H_pre+2). This is a full-domain implication on the\nstated branch, not an assumption about the relative order of the steps.\n\nThe two extensions therefore give explicit strict improvements in two\ncomplementary useful regimes: Section15 covers h0>sqrt(2), and this\nsection covers h0<=sqrt(2) with h1>=2. Neither result eliminates its\nwhole regime; they strengthen necessary tests there. Outside(47), the\nfull max(q_late,q_early) in(45) must be retained unless separately proved\ninactive. A positive remaining value of one witness is not a certificate.\n", "verifiable": true, "status": "unverified", "fact_id": null, "links": {"artifact": "workers/max2/research/four_step_seed_compact_domain.md Section16", "fact_ids": ["1252b8823bb5a290"], "related_findings": ["749c14575c110748", "81720922d2d132d7"], "status": "complete_joint_proof_draft_unverified", "new_optimizer": 0, "new_fact_submit": 0}, "glossary": {"a": "h0-1.", "b": "h1-1.", "c": "h2-1.", "d": "h3-1.", "C": "1+sum h.", "K_tail": "c²d+(c+d+2)(1-cd).", "r_tail": "c²d/K_tail.", "eta_tail": "c/(d+2).", "A_pre": "1+a+b.", "H_pre": "2+a+b.", "Z_tail": "(c+r_tail)/(1+d).", "q_late": "(1+(1-b)eta_tail)/b.", "q_early": "[eta_tail+(A_pre eta_tail-1)Z_tail]/A_pre.", "q_pre": "max(q_late,q_early).", "J_pre": "1-2H_pre eta_tail+H_pre² q_pre."}}

Repairing a quadratic prefix by the last triangle\n\nThere is a second full-pair way to use the same last triangle, which\ndoes not identify the first two auxiliary gradients. (48)\n\nHere is its direct construction. Base data have\n\n    x_base=(Q_pre*u,(2+b)*u,u,-c*u,w),\n    g_base=(u,u,u,v,w).\n\nAuxiliary data have\n\n    x_aux=(u,-a*u,a*b*u,-a*b*c*u,a*b*w),\n    g_aux=(u,-a*u,a*b*u,a*b*v,a*b*w).\n\nBoth arrays follow the actual h, and values are assigned in each array\nby f_i=<x_i,g_i>-||g_i||^2/2. The auxiliary normals vanish except at\nindex3, where the normal is a*b*n with n=-c*u-v. Write\n\n    k=<u,n>=(r_tail-c*d)/(1+d)<0.\n\nThe triangle gives <v,n>=-eta_tail*k>0 and <w,n>=<v,n>.\nAll nonzero auxiliary finite interpolation residuals have target3.\nFor sources0,1,2,4 respectively they are\n\n    a*b*k*(1+a*b*eta_tail) <0,\n    -a^2*b*k*(1-b*eta_tail) >0,\n    a^2*b^2*k*(1+eta_tail) <0,\n    0.\n\nThus the ONLY positive auxiliary pair is(1,3), since b*eta_tail<1.\nThe incoming optimum residual at3 is -a^2*b^2*<v,n><0 and the other\noptimum residuals are zero. The base array is legal by the triangle\nnormal check with all prefix nodes retained. Its pair(1,3) equals\n(1+eta_tail)*k<0.\n\nChoose\n\n    epsilon=(1+eta_tail)/(a^2*b*(1-b*eta_tail))>0.\n\nTake the orthogonal sum of base data and sqrt(epsilon) times auxiliary\ndata, with values added with weight epsilon. The pair(1,3) cancels\nexactly. Every other original finite or optimum residual is nonpositive.\nThe GD identities are preserved in both coordinates, so the complete\narray comes from one legal function by fact1252b8823bb5a290. Its energy is\n\n    Q_pre^2-C^2*r_tail\n       +epsilon*(1-C^2*a^2*b^2*r_tail).\n\nMultiplication by the positive a^2*b*(1-b*eta_tail) yields\n\n    a^2*b*(1-b*eta_tail)*Q_pre^2+(1+eta_tail)\n       -C^2*a^2*b*(1+b)*r_tail.\n\nIts nonnegativity is precisely(48). All auxiliaries are derived for the\nsame current schedule, with the actual first gradients u,-a*u,a*b*u.\n\nThe same repair can be made with the scalar last-cut base. In that\ncase take w=v=-eta_tail*u, x_3=(2+d)*w, x_4=w, and keep the same\nquadratic prefix in the auxiliary. The same residual calculation holds:\nits n=-(c-eta_tail)*u has k<0 and <v,n>=-eta_tail*k. This gives\n\n    C^2*eta_tail^2 <= J_quad,                        (49)\n\nwhich is the full j=2 cut repair in explicit rational coordinates,\nrederived here rather than adopted from an unverified finding.\n\nThe two independently legal functions can therefore be combined into\nthe stronger necessary scalar inequality\n\n    C^2*max(eta_tail^2,r_tail) <= J_quad.             (50)\n\nThe active branch has an exact schedule-independent description. Since\nK_tail=(c+d+2)-c*d*(d+2), direct multiplication gives\n\n    d*(d+2)^2-K_tail=(c+d+2)*(d*(d+2)-1).\n\nConsequently r_tail>eta_tail^2 exactly when d>sqrt(2)-1, with equality\nat that boundary. Thus(48) strictly strengthens the old scalar j=2\nrepair throughout h3>sqrt(2). On the complementary branch(49) is\nstronger. This is a strict comparison of necessary inequalities, not a\nclaim that either test is sufficient. The displayed functions themselves\nprove infeasibility whenever the relevant inequality is violated.\n\n## 18. {"id": "aedfcdbfe19373c6", "timestamp_utc": "2026-09-27T04:12:26.939270+00:00", "author": "max", "kind": "conclusion", "claim": "max24 UNVERIFIED exact value interface: after releasing projection heights, a positive cross normal must be paid by an ORIGINAL internal return-path cost.
